def compute_c_direct(h):
 n=len(h); m=2*n-1; dx=2.0/n; c=np.zeros(m); center=n-1
 for k in range(n): # k=0..n-1 positive
   nv=n-k
   c[center+k] = np.dot(h[:nv], 1.0 - h[k:]) * dx
   # negative lag -k: c[center-k] = dot(h[k:], 1-h[:nv]) * dx
   if k>0: c[center-k] = np.dot(h[k:], 1.0 - h[:nv]) * dx
 return c
Total n loops; each dot of arrays length average n/2 -> n^2/2 ops; for n=1024 ~524k? Actually sum nv = n(n+1)/2 ~524k, times two ->1M dot elements. Very fast. For n=2048 ~4M. Good. 2n loops with Python overhead 2048, fine. Use np.dot. This gives c in direct lag order index center + k. d mapping same.

def get_linear_d(h, i):
 n=len(h); m=2*n-1; center=n-1; d=np.zeros(m)
 # first: k=0..n-1-i
 if n-i>0:
   d[center:center+n-i] += 1.0 - h[i:]  # length n-i
 # second: k=0..i
 if i+1>0:
   d[center-i:center+1] -= h[i::-1]
 return d

So Δt= -δ(1-a)-bδ = -δ + aδ - bδ = δ(a - b -1). This equals L above. Good. Earlier I had +b sign wrong. So matches. Product change? New b' = b-δ, a'=a+δ: (b-δ)(1-a-δ)=b(1-a) -δ(1-a) -bδ = ... yes.
Quadratic: -ΔpΔq = -(-δ*δ)=+δ^2. good.

Thus pair update formula correct.

def pair_candidate_delta(c,L,idx,Q,lo,hi,Vhi,feas_intervals):
 candidates=[]
 add 0, lo, hi
 for interval: add l,r,mid
 evaluate:
   best_delta=0; best_val=current_max (passed)
   for delta in candidates:
      val = np.max(c + delta*L)
      for each special: qval = c[idx]+delta*L[idx]+Q[idx]*delta*delta; if qval>val: val=qval
      if val < best_val: best_val=val; best_delta=delta
   return best_delta,best_val

pair_update function signature:
def exact_pair_update(h, c, i, b, tol=1e-13):
    n = len(h); m = 2*n -1; center = n-1
    hi_val = h[i]; hb_val = h[b]
    lo = max(-hi_val, hb_val - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - hi_val, hb_val)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
        return 0.0, float(np.max(c))
    # Compute L
    # Could compute d_i and d_b with get_linear_d copied arrays. We can compute L directly more efficiently without separate d? L(k)=d_i(k)-d_b(k). We can build L zeros and add contributions:
    # L for i: first range center..center+n-1-i +=1-h[i:]
    #              second center-i..center += h[i::-1]
    # L for b subtract:
    # L[center..center+n-1-b] -= 1-h[b:]
    # L[center-b..center] -= h[b::-1]  (because d_b second subtract negative? Let's do:
    # d_a: first +A, second -A
    # d_b: first +B, second -B
    # L=d_a-d_b => first +A -B; second -A +B.
    # So:
    L = np.zeros(m)
    # for a first
    L[center:center+n-i] += (1-h[i:])
    # for a second
    L[center-i:center+1] -= h[i::-1]
    # subtract b first
    L[center:center+n-b] -= (1-h[b:])
    # subtract b second: - ( - h[b::-1] ) = + h[b::-1]
    L[center-b:center+1] += h[b::-1]
    This avoids two arrays. Need careful if i<b, ranges overlap and add. This cost 4 slice operations length n. Good.
    # Special indices: idx0=center; Q0=-2. If abs(b-i)>0: s=b-i; idx_pos=center+s, idx_neg=center-s; Q=+1. (If s=0 skip). Note if idx_pos==center? no.
    # Build affine pos/neg/zero_idx once:
    # special_mask maybe we can mark bool array zeros(m) and set specs. m=1000, allocation okay. Or manually exclude specs by using L and a small list check? In vector masks, easiest special_mask.
    spec_mask = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
    spec_mask[center] = True
    if i != b:
       spec_mask[center + (b-i)] = True
       spec_mask[center - (b-i)] = True
    nz = np.abs(L) > 1e-14
    aff = nz & (~spec_mask)
    pos_idx = np.nonzero(aff & (L > 0.0))[0]
    neg_idx = np.nonzero(aff & (L < 0.0))[0]
    zero_mask = (~nz) & (~spec_mask)
    if np.any(zero_mask): const_c = float(np.max(c[zero_mask])) else const_c = 0? If no zero, -inf.
    # Precompute current_max = float(np.max(c))
    # Feasible function inline maybe as top-level requiring many args. We'll define top-level feasible_intervals(...) and call.
    Vlo=0.0; Vhi=current_max
    # Maybe if current_max - Vlo small? At least.
    # Binary:
    delta_best=0.0; best_val=current_max
    feas_intervals = feasible_intervals(Vhi, lo, hi, pos_idx, neg_idx, const_c, c, L, spec_idx_list, spec_Q_list) maybe should be feasible.
    for it in range(20):
       if Vhi - Vlo <= 1e-12*(1+Vhi): break
       mid=(Vlo+Vhi)*0.5
       intervals = feasible_intervals(mid, ...)
       if intervals is not None:
          Vhi=mid
       else:
          Vlo=mid
    # After binary, get intervals at Vhi+1e-12 maybe
    intervals=feasible_intervals(Vhi+1e-12,...)
    if intervals is None: intervals=feasible_intervals(Vhi+1e-9,...)
    # Candidate deltas:
    cand = [0.0, lo, hi]
    for l,r in intervals: cand += [l,r,(l+r)*0.5]
    # Also maybe roots? intervals endpoints include roots if special tight.
    # Evaluate candidates:
    for d in cand:
       if d < lo: d=lo; if d>hi: d=hi
       val = np.max(c + d*L)
       # adjust spec
       for idx,q in zip(spec_idx_list, spec_Q_list):
           qv = c[idx] + d*L[idx] + q*d*d
           if qv > val: val=qv
       if val < best_val - 1e-14:
           best_val=val; delta_best=d
    # If best delta improves, apply:
    if abs(delta_best)>1e-14 and best_val < current_max - 1e-13:
       apply_pair_delta(h,c,i,b,delta_best)
       return delta_best, best_val
    else:
       return 0, current_max
Return improvement? We'll have h,c mutated if improved.

Feasible_intervals top-level:
def feasible_intervals(V, lo, hi, pos_idx, neg_idx, const_c, c, L, spec_idx, spec_Q):
    if const_c > V + 1e-12:
       return None
    Lb=lo; Ub=hi
    if Lb > Ub + 1e-13: return None
    if pos_idx.size:
       ub = (V - c[pos_idx]) / L[pos_idx]
       Ub = min(Ub, float(ub.max()))
    if neg_idx.size:
       lb = (V - c[neg_idx]) / L[neg_idx]
       Lb = max(Lb, float(lb.max()))
    if Lb > Ub + 1e-12:
       return None
    if Lb < lo: Lb=lo
    if Ub > hi: Ub=hi
    if Lb > Ub + 1e-12: return None
    intervals = [(float(Lb), float(Ub))]
    for idx,q,B? Actually B=L[idx], C=c[idx]-V.
       A=q
       qq = feasible_quad_interval...
       new=[]
       for (l,r) in intervals:
         ...

def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(np.array(h,dtype=float),0,1)
 target=n/2.0
 for _ in range(10000):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:
     room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); if tot<1e-15: break; h += diff/tot*room; h=np.minimum(h,1)
   else:
     tot=h.sum(); if tot<1e-15: break; h += diff/tot*h; h=np.maximum(h,0)

Local_search logic:
def local_search(h, c, current_max, deadline, rng, min_time=20):
  n=len(h)
  total_improv=0
  # slack passes
  # Get slacks based on current h: indices sorted by h*(1-h) desc, take unique top maybe 6 plus random.
  slacks = choose_slack_list(h, rng, count=6)
  last_improv = current_max - 1? 
  for slack in slacks:
    if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
    # For each slack, up to 3 passes or until no improvement
    for pass in range(4):
      if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
       # maybe choose a new slack if b moved to boundary? The fixed slack is index; if h[b] near boundary after pass, may be less effective. If count>1, list from initial; okay.
      improved = fixed_slack_pass(h,c,slack, deadline-40, rng)
      # after each pass, project/recompute
      h = project_feasible(h,n); c=compute_c_direct(h); current_max=float(np.max(c))
      total_improv += improved
      if improved == 0: break
  # Random pairs if time
  if time.time() < deadline - 20:
    rand_deadline = min(deadline-5, time.time()+ max(20, (deadline-time.time())*0.3))
    current_max = random_pair_search(h,c,current_max,rand_deadline,rng,n)
    h=project; c=compute; current_max=max
  return h,c,current_max

But fixed_slack_pass should recompute? It can not recompute each update; after pass yes outside. During pass, if current_max decreases, continues. If a pass has no pair improvement, it breaks.

fixed_slack_pass:
def fixed_slack_pass(h,c,b,deadline,rng):
 n=len(h)
 indices=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(indices) # but if indices includes b, skip
 improved=0
 for i in indices:
  if i==b: continue
  if time.time()>deadline: break
  old_max = float(np.max(c))? exact_pair computes. To reduce copy, pass current_max? We'll maintain current_max inside pass. But exact_pair computes current_max anyway. Maybe outside pass current_max known. For update, pair_update returns new_max.
  current_max = current_max parameter. In function:
   cm = current_max
   for i:
      new_max, updated = exact_pair_update(h,c,i,b,cm)
      if updated:

Now choose_slack_list:
def choose_slack_list(h, rng, count=6):
 n=len(h); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 score = h*(1-h)
 # top indices
 idx=np.argsort(-score)
 choices=[]
 # Ensure interior >1e-3; else all.
 for idx_i in idx:
   if score[idx_i] < 1e-4 and len(choices)<count:
       # if few interior, still choose top
   if len(choices)>=count: break
   choices.append(int(idx_i))
 # add random top 50
 top50=idx[:min(50,n)]
 for _ in range(count-len(choices)):
   if len(top50)>0: choices.append(int(rng.choice(top50)))
 # unique
 dedup
 return choices

def feasible_intervals(V, lo, hi, pos_idx, neg_idx, const_c, c, L, spec_idx, spec_q):
    tol = 1e-12
    if const_c > V + tol:
        return None
    Lb = float(lo); Ub = float(hi)
    if Lb > Ub + tol: return None
    psize = len(pos_idx)
    if psize:
        pv = (V - c[pos_idx]) / L[pos_idx]
        m = float(np.max(pv))
        if m < Ub: Ub = m
    nsize = len(neg_idx)
    if nsize:
        nv = (V - c[neg_idx]) / L[neg_idx]
        m = float(np.max(nv))
        if m > Lb: Lb = m
    if Lb < lo: Lb = lo
    if Ub > hi: Ub = hi
    if Lb > Ub + tol: return None
    intervals = [(Lb, Ub)]
    for t in range(len(spec_idx)):
        idx = spec_idx[t]; A = spec_q[t]; B = float(L[idx]); C = float(c[idx]) - V
        new_intervals=[]
        for (l,r) in intervals:
            l=float(l); r=float(r)
            if l > r + tol: continue
            if A > 1e-14:
                D = B*B - 4.0*A*C
                if D < -1e-12:
                    continue
                if D < 0.0: D=0.0
                s=np.sqrt(D)
                den=2.0*A
                r1=(-B - s)/den; r2=(-B + s)/den
                nl = l if l > r1 else r1
                nr = r if r < r2 else r2
                if nl <= nr + tol:
                    new_intervals.append((nl,nr))
            elif A < -1e-14:
                D = B*B - 4.0*A*C
                if D < -1e-12:
                    # always satisfied
                    new_intervals.append((l,r))
                else:
                    if D < 0.0: D=0.0
                    s=np.sqrt(D); den=2.0*A
                    r1=(-B - s)/den; r2=(-B + s)/den
                    if r1 > r2:
                        tmp=r1; r1=r2; r2=tmp
                    # outside [r1,r2]
                    nr = r if r < r1 else r1
                    if l <= nr + tol:
                        new_intervals.append((l, nr))
                    nl = l if l > r2 else r2
                    if nl <= r + tol:
                        new_intervals.append((nl, r))
            else:
                if abs(B) <= 1e-14:
                    if float(c[idx]) <= V + tol:
                        new_intervals.append((l,r))
                else:
                    if B > 0:
                        nr = r if r < (V - float(c[idx]))/B else (V - float(c[idx]))/B
                        if l <= nr + tol:
                            new_intervals.append((l,nr))
                    else:
                        nl = l if l > (V - float(c[idx]))/B else (V - float(c[idx]))/B
                        if nl <= r + tol:
                            new_intervals.append((nl,r))
        if not new_intervals:
            return None
        # merge intervals
        intervals.sort(key lambda? No lambdas allowed. Cannot use lambda. We need sort top-level without lambda? Python list sort key must be callable; lambda not allowed. We can avoid merging or use intervals.sort() because tuples sort by first then second. Tuples (l,r), intervals.sort() works, no lambda. Good. But key not needed.
        merged=[]
        for iv in intervals:
           if not merged or iv[0] > merged[-1][1] + tol:
              merged.append(iv)
           elif iv[1] > merged[-1][1]:
              merged[-1]=(merged[-1][0], iv[1])
        intervals=merged
    return intervals

def exact_pair_update(h, c, i, b, current_max):
    n=len(h); m=2*n-1; center=n-1

    L = np.empty(m) ??? zeros. Use np.zeros(m)

Compute_c_direct using loops:
def compute_c_direct(h):
    n=h.size; m=2*n-1; dx=2.0/n; c=np.empty(m); center=n-1
    # k=0
    c[center]=np.dot(h, 1.0-h)*dx
    for k in range(1,n):
       nv=n-k
       c[center+k]=np.dot(h[:nv], 1.0-h[k:])*dx
       c[center-k]=np.dot(h[k:], 1.0-h[:nv])*dx
    return c
Could vectorize k=0 inside loop? This loop has n-1 iterations, each dot. For n=2048, 2047 iterations. Good.

But np.dot of slices with 1-h each time creates 1-h? 1.0-h[k:] creates view? It creates array? Actually 1.0 - h[k:] makes copy of length nv. That's okay. Could optimize by precompute v=1-h and dot(h[:nv], v[k:]) etc. Let's:
 v = 1.0 - h
 c[center]=np.dot(h,v)*dx
 for k: c[center+k]=np.dot(h[:n-k], v[k:])*dx; c[center-k]=np.dot(h[k:], v[:n-k])*dx
But for negative lag, c_direct[-k]=∑ h_{i+k}(1-h_i) = dot(h[k:], v[:n-k]). Good.
This avoids 1-h repeated. Good.

Project_feasible with final exact adjustment:
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(200):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<5e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<=1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
          h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<=1e-15: break
          h += (diff/tot)*h
          h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # exact adjust at one or more interior
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # choose indices with fraction far from bounds, distribute
       idx=np.argsort(-(h*(1-h)))
       for j in range(min(n, len(idx))):
          if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
          idx_j=int(idx[j]); old=h[idx_j]
          val=old+diff
          if 0.0 <= val <= 1.0:
             h[idx_j]=val; diff=0.0
          else:
             # move as much as possible
             if diff>0:
                add=1.0-old
             else:
                add=-old
             h[idx_j]=old+add; diff -= add
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
This final exact may push multiple. Good.

But if after pair updates h sum drifts >1e-12, project might change many values and objective. It should be tiny.

If not, unchanged.

fixed_slack_pass:
def fixed_slack_pass(h, c, b, deadline, rng, max_updates=None):
    n=len(h)
    if max_updates is None:
       indices=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(indices)
    # maintain cm
    cm = float(np.max(c))? We should not recompute? But pass can accept cm external. Maybe exact_pair_update doesn't accept? We'll adjust signature exact_pair_update(h,c,i,b,current_max). It uses current_max. In fixed pass, pass cm in.
    improved=0
    stale=0
    for i in indices:
       if int(i)==b: continue
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       old=cm
       cm, upd = exact_pair_update(h,c,int(i),b,cm)
       if upd:
          improved +=1; stale=0
          if max_updates and improved>=max_updates: break
       else:
          stale+=1
          if stale > n*3: # maybe break if many no improvement? But fixed slack should continue? If many no improvements, likely local for this slack. We can break after e.g. 2*n consecutive no update.
              # In a shuffled pass, if long streak, maybe near convergence.
              # But useful to complete pass? Could break to choose new slack.
              # break after 300? We'll use 2*n.
              if stale > max(200, 2*n):
                  break
    return improved, cm
But if we break early, not full pass; improved maybe >0. Good.

Implement local_search:
def local_search(h, c, cm, deadline, rng):
    n=len(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_cm=cm
    # fixed slack passes: choose slacks from best_h? If current changes, choose once.
    slacks = choose_slack_list(best_h, rng, count=5)
    # To allow slack move, after each slack pass if no improvement, maybe choose a new slack based on current
    pass_count=0
    no_improve_slacks=0
    for slack in slacks:
       if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
       for _ in range(3):
          if time.time() > deadline - 40: break
          improved, cm = fixed_slack_pass(h,c,slack, deadline-50, rng)
          h,c,cm = refresh(h,c)
          if cm < best_cm - 1e-12:
             best_cm=cm; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
          if improved == 0:
             break
          pass_count +=1
          # if slack now at boundary? need not
       if time.time() > deadline - 60: break
    # restore current to best if current_cm much worse?
    if cm > best_cm + 1e-9:
       h=best_h.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); cm=best_cm
    # random pair if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
       rand_dead = deadline - 10
       # Limit max updates depending n: maybe min(5000, 20*n?) but time govern.
       cm = random_pair_search(h,c,cm,rand_dead,rng,n)
       h,c,cm=refresh(h,c)
       if cm < best_cm -1e-12: update best
    # final fixed slack pass on best if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       slacks2 = choose_slack_list(best_h, rng, count=3)
       for slack in slacks2:
          if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
          improved, cm = fixed_slack_pass(best_h,best_c,slack, deadline-10, rng)
          # Need if best_h/c mutated; fixed pass mutates best variables. If we use best_h directly, okay. But if not improved, update? It may still mutate with no improvement? exact_pair only mutates on improvement. So fine.
          best_h,best_c,cm2=refresh(best_h,best_c)
          if cm2 < best_cm: best_cm=cm2
          if improved==0: break
    return best_h,best_c,best_cm

random_pair_search:
def random_pair_search(h,c,cm,deadline,rng,n):
    stale=0
    # Choose number of attempts? loop time.
    while time.time() < deadline:
       i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
       if i==j: continue
       old=cm
       cm, upd = exact_pair_update(h,c,i,j,cm)
       if upd: stale=0
       else:
          stale +=1
          if stale > 500:
             break
    return cm
This may run until deadline; in local_search we set deadline-10. Good. But if exact_pair_update expensive and n=1024, it will stop by time.
Could allow occasional worsening? Not.

Pseudo run:
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
 try:
   h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
 except Exception:
   # fallback: maybe start with half 0.5? Need sum n/2. n=500 h0=0.5 all? C=0.5. Or better create block pattern? Parent likely.
   n0=500; h0=np.full(n0,0.5)
 h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
 c0=compute_c_direct(h0); best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=c0.copy(); best_cm=float(np.max(c0)); best_n=n0
 # Phase 1: local search on parent with generous time
 phase1_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 600) # 600s if budget 1100
 h1,c1,cm1 = local_search(h0.copy(),c0.copy(),best_cm, phase1_deadline, rng)
 if cm1 < best_cm:
    best_cm=cm1; best_h=h1.copy(); best_c=c1.copy(); best_n=n1=len(h1)
 # If time left, try upsample best to higher n
 if time.time() < deadline - 250:
    target_n = 1024 if best_n < 1024 else best_n*2
    # maybe if best_n > 1024 skip high. If parent 500, choose 1024.
    h_up=upsample_h(best_h,best_n,target_n)
    c_up=compute_c_direct(h_up); cm_up=float(np.max(c_up))
    # Only if not much worse. But even if worse, could improve below best after local.
    if cm_up < best_cm + 0.02 and time.time() < deadline - 200:
       h_u,c_u,cm_u=local_search(h_up,c_up,cm_up, deadline-80, rng)
       if cm_u < best_cm:
          best_cm=cm_u; best_h=h_u.copy(); best_c=c_u.copy(); best_n=len(h_u)
    # If n_up improved, maybe upsample further to 2048 if time >300
    if time.time() < deadline - 300 and best_n < 2048:
       target_n2=2048
       h_up2=upsample_h(best_h,best_n,target_n2)
       c_up2=compute_c_direct(h_up2); cm_up2=float(np.max(c_up2))
       if cm_up2 < best_cm + 0.02:
          h_u2,c_u2,cm_u2=local_search(h_up2,c_up2,cm_up2, deadline-40, rng)
          if cm_u2 < best_cm: update
 # Final: if time left, local search on current best until deadline-5
 if time.time() < deadline - 20:
    h_f,c_f,cm_f=local_search(best_h.copy(),best_c.copy(),best_cm, deadline-2, rng)
    if cm_f < best_cm: best_cm=cm_f; best_h=h_f.copy(); best_n=len(h_f)
 # Final project and evaluate exact with compute_c_direct (or np.correlate? use direct max same)
 final_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
 final_c=compute_c_direct(final_h)
 final_cm=float(np.max(final_c))
 # It might be slightly different; set best
 return final_h, final_cm, best_n

def upsample_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    dx_old=2.0/n_old; dx_new=2.0/n_new
    x_old = np.arange(n_old)*dx_old + 0.5*dx_old
    x_new = np.arange(n_new)*dx_new + 0.5*dx_new
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old, left=h_old[0], right=h_old[-1])
    return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)
For x_new near 2, last center <2. Good. If n_new close, fine.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

DEFAULT_BINARY_ITERS = 18


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = n / 2.0

    for _ in range(300):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 5e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        order = np.argsort(-(h * (1.0 - h)))
        for jj in range(min(n, int(order.size))):
            if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
                break
            ii = int(order[jj])
            old = float(h[ii])
            if 0.0 <= old + diff <= 1.0:
                h[ii] = old + diff
                break
            if diff > 0.0:
                add = 1.0 - old
            else:
                add = -old
            h[ii] = old + add
            diff -= add

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def compute_c_direct(h):
    n = int(h.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([0.0])
    m = 2 * n - 1
    center = n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    v = 1.0 - h
    c = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    c[center] = float(np.dot(h, v)) * dx
    for k in range(1, n):
        nv = n - k
        c[center + k] = float(np.dot(h[:nv], v[k:])) * dx
        c[center - k] = float(np.dot(h[k:], v[:nv])) * dx
    return c


def resample_h(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    n_old = int(n_old)
    n_new = max(2, int(n_new))
    if n_old == n_new or n_new <= 2:
        return project_feasible(h_old, n_new)
    dx_old = 2.0 / float(n_old)
    dx_new = 2.0 / float(n_new)
    x_old = np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) * dx_old + 0.5 * dx_old
    x_new = np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) * dx_new + 0.5 * dx_new
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old, left=float(h_old[0]), right=float(h_old[-1]))
    return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)


def choose_slack_list(h, rng, count=5):
    n = int(h.size)
    count = max(1, min(int(count), n))
    score = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0) * (1.0 - np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0))
    order = np.argsort(-score)
    top_n = min(n, max(count, 3 * count))
    top = order[:top_n].copy()
    rng.shuffle(top)
    choices = []
    for x in top:
        xi = int(x)
        if xi not in choices:
            choices.append(xi)
            if len(choices) >= count:
                return choices
    for x in order[top_n:]:
        xi = int(x)
        if xi not in choices:
            choices.append(xi)
            if len(choices) >= count:
                break
    return choices


def feasible_intervals(V, lo, hi, pos_idx, neg_idx, const_c, c, L, spec_idx, spec_q):
    tol = 1e-12
    if const_c > V + tol:
        return None

    Lb = float(lo)
    Ub = float(hi)
    if Lb > Ub + tol:
        return None

    if pos_idx.size:
        tmp = (V - c[pos_idx]) / L[pos_idx]
        mm = float(np.max(tmp))
        if mm < Ub:
            Ub = mm

    if neg_idx.size:
        tmp = (V - c[neg_idx]) / L[neg_idx]
        mm = float(np.max(tmp))
        if mm > Lb:
            Lb = mm

    if Lb < lo:
        Lb = float(lo)
    if Ub > hi:
        Ub = float(hi)
    if Lb > Ub + tol:
        return None

    intervals = [(Lb, Ub)]

    for t in range(len(spec_idx)):
        idx = int(spec_idx[t])
        A = float(spec_q[t])
        B = float(L[idx])
        C = float(c[idx]) - V
        new_intervals = []

        for (l, r) in intervals:
            l = float(l)
            r = float(r)
            if l > r + tol:
                continue

            if A > 1e-14:
                D = B * B - 4.0 * A * C
                if D < -1e-12:
                    continue
                if D < 0.0:
                    D = 0.0
                s = float(np.sqrt(D))
                den = 2.0 * A
                r1 = (-B - s) / den
                r2 = (-B + s) / den
                nl = l if l > r1 else r1
                nr = r if r < r2 else r2
                if nl <= nr + tol:
                    new_intervals.append((nl, nr))

            elif A < -1e-14:
                D = B * B - 4.0 * A * C
                if D < -1e-12:
                    new_intervals.append((l, r))
                else:
                    if D < 0.0:
                        D = 0.0
                    s = float(np.sqrt(D))
                    den = 2.0 * A
                    r1 = (-B - s) / den
                    r2 = (-B + s) / den
                    if r1 > r2:
                        tmp = r1
                        r1 = r2
                        r2 = tmp

                    nr = r if r < r1 else r1
                    if l <= nr + tol:
                        new_intervals.append((l, nr))

                    nl = l if l > r2 else r2
                    if nl <= r + tol:
                        new_intervals.append((nl, r))

            else:
                if abs(B) <= 1e-14:
                    if float(c[idx]) <= V + tol:
                        new_intervals.append((l, r))
                else:
                    if B > 0.0:
                        limit = (V - float(c[idx])) / B
                        nr = r if r < limit else limit
                        if l <= nr + tol:
                            new_intervals.append((l, nr))
                    else:
                        limit = (V - float(c[idx])) / B
                        nl = l if l > limit else limit
                        if nl <= r + tol:
                            new_intervals.append((nl, r))

        if not new_intervals:
            return None

        new_intervals.sort()
        merged = []
        for iv in new_intervals:
            ivl = float(iv[0])
           ivr = float(iv[1])
            if ivl > ivr + tol:
                continue
            if not merged or ivl > merged[-1][1] + tol:
                merged.append((ivl, ivr))
            elif ivr > merged[-1][1]:
                merged[-1] = (merged[-1][0], ivr)
        intervals = merged

    return intervals


def exact_pair_update(h, c, i, b):
    n = int(h.size)
    if i == b or n < 2:
        return float(np.max(c)), False

    i = int(i)
    b = int(b)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    center = n - 1
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    cur_max = float(np.max(c))

    hi_val = float(h[i])
    hb_val = float(h[b])
    lo = max(-hi_val, hb_val - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - hi_val, hb_val)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-13:
        return cur_max, False

    Lraw = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    one_minus_h = 1.0 - h
    rev_h = h[::-1]

    Lraw[(n - 1) - i : (n - 1) - i + n] += one_minus_h
    Lraw[i : n + i] -= rev_h

    Lraw[(n - 1) - b : (n - 1) - b + n] -= one_minus_h
    Lraw[b : n + b] += rev_h

    L = Lraw * dx

    s = b - i
    spec_idx = [center]
    spec_q = [-2.0 * dx]
    if s != 0:
        spec_idx.append(center + s)
        spec_idx.append(center - s)
        spec_q.extend([dx, dx])

    spec_mask = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
    for idx in spec_idx:
        spec_mask[int(idx)] = True

    tolL = 1e-15
    pos_idx = np.nonzero((L > tolL) & (~spec_mask))[0]
    neg_idx = np.nonzero((L < -tolL) & (~spec_mask))[0]
    zero_mask = (L <= tolL) & (L >= -tolL)
    zero_mask = zero_mask & (~spec_mask)

    if zero_mask.any():
        const_c = float(np.max(c[zero_mask]))
    else:
        const_c = 0.0

    Vlo = max(0.0, const_c)
    if Vlo > cur_max + 1e-12:
        Vlo = cur_max
    if cur_max - Vlo <= 1e-13:
        return cur_max, False

    Vhi = cur_max

    intervals = None
    for _ in range(DEFAULT_BINARY_ITERS):
        if Vhi - Vlo <= 1e-11 * max(1.0, Vhi):
            break
        mid = 0.5 * (Vlo + Vhi)
        intervals = feasible_intervals(mid, lo, hi, pos_idx, neg_idx, const_c, c, L, spec_idx, spec_q)
        if intervals is not None:
            Vhi = mid
        else:
            Vlo = mid

    eps = max(1e-11, 1e-9 * max(1.0, cur_max - Vlo))
    intervals = feasible_intervals(Vhi + eps, lo, hi, pos_idx, neg_idx, const_c, c, L, spec_idx, spec_q)
    if intervals is None:
        intervals = feasible_intervals(Vhi + max(1e-9, 1e-7 * max(1.0, cur_max)), lo, hi, pos_idx, neg_idx, const_c, c, L, spec_idx, spec_q)
    if intervals is None:
        return cur_max, False

    candidates = [0.0, lo, hi]
    for iv in intervals:
        candidates.append(float(iv[0]))
        candidates.append(float(iv[1]))
        candidates.append(0.5 * (float(iv[0]) + float(iv[1])))

    best_delta = 0.0
    best_val = cur_max

    for d in candidates:
        d = float(d)
        if d < lo - 1e-12 or d > hi + 1e-12:
            continue
        if d < lo:
            d = lo
        if d > hi:
            d = hi

        if abs(d) <= 1e-14:
            val = cur_max
        else:
            val_arr = c + d * L
            val_arr[center] = -1e100
            val = float(np.max(val_arr))
            for t in range(len(spec_idx)):
                idx = int(spec_idx[t])
                q = float(spec_q[t])
                qv = float(c[idx] + d * L[idx] + q * d * d)
                if qv > val:
                    val = qv

        if val < best_val - 1e-13:
            best_val = val
            best_delta = d

    if best_delta != 0.0 and best_val < cur_max - 1e-12:
        dd = best_delta * best_delta
        c += best_delta * L
        c[center] -= 2.0 * dx * dd
        if s != 0:
            c[center + s] += dx * dd
            c[center - s] += dx * dd

        h[i] += best_delta
        h[b] -= best_delta

        if h[i] < 0.0 and h[i] > -1e-12:
            h[i] = 0.0
        elif h[i] > 1.0 and h[i] < 1.0 + 1e-12:
            h[i] = 1.0
        if h[b] < 0.0 and h[b] > -1e-12:
            h[b] = 0.0
        elif h[b] > 1.0 and h[b] < 1.0 + 1e-12:
            h[b] = 1.0

        return float(best_val), True

    return cur_max, False


def refresh_state(h, c):
    n = int(h.size)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c_direct(h)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    return h, c, cm


def fixed_slack_pass(h, c, b, deadline, rng):
    n = int(h.size)
    b = int(b)
    if n < 2 or time.time() >= deadline:
        return 0, float(np.max(c))

    indices = np.arange(n)
    rng.shuffle(indices)

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    improved = 0
    stale = 0
    stale_limit = max(300, 2 * n)

    for ii in indices:
        i = int(ii)
        if i == b:
            continue
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        new_cm, updated = exact_pair_update(h, c, i, b)
        cm = new_cm
        if updated:
            improved += 1
            stale = 0
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= stale_limit:
                break

    return improved, cm


def random_pair_search(h, c, deadline, rng, max_updates=None):
    n = int(h.size)
    if n < 2 or time.time() >= deadline:
        return float(np.max(c))

    if max_updates is None:
        if n <= 700:
            max_updates = 12000
        elif n <= 1500:
            max_updates = 3500
        else:
            max_updates = 1200

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    stale = 0
    stale_limit = max(200, max_updates // 20)

    for _ in range(int(max_updates)):
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
        if i == j:
            stale += 1
        else:
            new_cm, updated = exact_pair_update(h, c, i, j)
            cm = new_cm
            if updated:
                stale = 0
            else:
                stale += 1
        if stale > stale_limit:
            break

    return cm


def local_search(h, c, cm, deadline, rng):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = np.array(c, dtype=float).copy()
    cm = float(cm)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_cm = cm

    if time.time() >= deadline - 30:
        return best_h, best_c, best_cm

    slacks = choose_slack_list(h, rng, count=5)
    pass_budget_exhausted = time.time() > deadline - 60

    for slack in slacks:
        if time.time() > deadline - 40:
            break
        for _ in range(4):
            if time.time() > deadline - 50:
                pass_budget_exhausted = True
                break
            improved, cm = fixed_slack_pass(h, c, int(slack), deadline - 55, rng)
            h, c, cm = refresh_state(h, c)

            if cm < best_cm - 1e-12:
                best_cm = cm
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
            elif cm > best_cm + 1e-7:
                h = best_h.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                cm = best_cm

            if improved == 0:
                break

    if time.time() > deadline - 60:
        pass_budget_exhausted = True

    if not pass_budget_exhausted and time.time() < deadline - 25:
        cm = random_pair_search(h, c, deadline - 12, rng)
        h, c, cm = refresh_state(h, c)
        if cm < best_cm - 1e-12:
            best_cm = cm
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()
        elif cm > best_cm + 1e-7:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            cm = best_cm

    if time.time() < deadline - 18:
        slacks2 = choose_slack_list(best_h, rng, count=3)
        for slack in slacks2:
            if time.time() > deadline - 10:
                break
            h_try = best_h.copy()
            c_try = best_c.copy()
            cm_try = best_cm
            improved, cm_try = fixed_slack_pass(h_try, c_try, int(slack), deadline - 10, rng)
            h_try, c_try, cm_try = refresh_state(h_try, c_try)
            if cm_try < best_cm - 1e-12:
                best_cm = cm_try
                best_h = h_try.copy()
                best_c = c_try.copy()
            if improved == 0:
                break

    return best_h, best_c, float(best_cm)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 6.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(h0.size)
        if n0 < 2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h0)):
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        n0 = 500
        rr = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        h0 = rr.integers(0, 2, size=n0).astype(float)

    if n0 > 2048:
        h0 = resample_h(h0, n0, 2048)
        n0 = int(h0.size)

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    c0 = compute_c_direct(h0)
    cm0 = float(np.max(c0))

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = c0.copy()
    best_cm = cm0
    best_n = n0

    reserve1 = max(180.0, 0.25 * budget)
    end1 = min(deadline - reserve1, t0 + 0.58 * budget)
    end1 = max(t0 + 30.0, end1)

    if end1 > t0 + 35.0:
        h1, c1, cm1 = local_search(h0.copy(), c0.copy(), cm0, end1, rng)
        if cm1 < best_cm - 1e-12:
            best_cm = cm1
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_c = c1.copy()
            best_n = int(h1.size)

    if time.time() < deadline - 220:
        target_n = 1024
        if best_n >= 1024:
            target_n = min(2048, 2 * best_n)
        if target_n > best_n:
            h_up = resample_h(best_h, best_n, target_n)
            c_up = compute_c_direct(h_up)
            cm_up = float(np.max(c_up))
            if cm_up < best_cm + 0.02 and time.time() < deadline - 200:
                end2 = deadline - 130.0
                h2, c2, cm2 = local_search(h_up, c_up, cm_up, end2, rng)
                if cm2 < best_cm - 1e-12:
                    best_cm = cm2
                    best_h = h2.copy()
                    best_c = c2.copy()
                    best_n = int(h2.size)

    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
        hf, cf, cmf = local_search(best_h.copy(), best_c.copy(), best_cm, deadline - 2.0, rng)
        if cmf < best_cm - 1e-12:
            best_cm = cmf
            best_h = hf.copy()
            best_c = cf.copy()
            best_n = int(hf.size)

    final_n = int(best_h.size)
    final_h = project_feasible(best_h, final_n)

    if final_n == 0:
        final_n = 500
        final_h = np.full(final_n, 0.5)
        final_h = project_feasible(final_h, final_n)

    final_c5 = float(np.max(np.correlate(final_h, 1.0 - final_h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / float(final_n)))

    return final_h, final_c5, final_n
```
